It does not refer to any one specific legal entity. All services are provided by one or more of the specific Anchorage Digital entities. Custody, settlement, staking, and governance services are offered through Anchorage Digital Bank National Association (“Anchorage Digital Bank”). Digital asset trading services are provided by Anchorage Hold LLC (“Anchorage Hold”). Agency trading services are offered in New York by Anchorage Digital NY, LLC. BitLicense #0000041. A1 Ltd. is a principal trading business. Anchorage Services, LLC (“Anchorage Services”) is an NFA-registered introducing broker, NFA ID No. 0532710. Anchorage Digital Bank, Anchorage Hold, and Anchorage Services are not registered with the SEC or any state authority as a broker or dealer and are not authorized to engage in the business of the offer, sale, or trading of securities. Anchorage Digital services are offered to institutions and certain high net worth individuals in limited circumstances. Certain trading services are designed and available only for institutions who meet eligibility requirements, including qualification as an Eligible Contract Participant (ECP) under the rules of the U.S. Commodity Futures Trading Commission. For institutions participating in custody, staking, or governance with Anchorage’s Singapore entity, those services are offered through Anchorage Digital Singapore Pte Ltd (“Anchorage Digital Singapore”). <script>
/** * 1. UTM Attribution Logic (Immediate)
 * Fills hidden form fields with tracking data as soon as the page is ready.
 */
function syncUTMs() {
  const my_utmParameters = ["gclid", "twclid", "rdt_cid", "cls", "utm_source", "utm_medium", "utm_campaign", "utm_term", "utm_content"];
  const urlParams = Object.fromEntries(new URLSearchParams(location.search));
  const cookieExist = Cookies.get('Lead');

  if (Object.keys(urlParams).length > 0 && typeof cookieExist === 'undefined') {
    Cookies.set('Lead', JSON.stringify({ parameters: urlParams }), { expires: 1 });
  }

  const currentLead = Cookies.get('Lead');
  if (currentLead) {
    const params = JSON.parse(currentLead).parameters;
    my_utmParameters.forEach(p => {
      const nodes = document.getElementsByClassName(p);
      for (let i = 0; i < nodes.length; i++) {
        nodes[i].value = params[p] || "null";
      }
    });
  }
}

/** * 2. Secondary UI Tasks (Delayed)
 */
function runSecondaryTasks() {
  if (window.tasksInitialized) return;
  window.tasksInitialized = true;

  if (document.querySelector(".primary-testimonials--collection-list-wrapper")) {
    new Swiper(".primary-testimonials--collection-list-wrapper", {
      wrapperClass: "primary-testimonials--collection-list",
      slidesPerView: 1,
      navigation: { nextEl: ".testimonial-right-arrow", prevEl: ".testimonial-left-arrow" },
      breakpoints: { 768: { slidesPerView: "auto" } }
    });
  }
}

// Global Execution
window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  syncUTMs(); 
  
  window.addEventListener('scroll', runSecondaryTasks, { once: true });
  window.addEventListener('mousemove', runSecondaryTasks, { once: true });
  setTimeout(runSecondaryTasks, 2000); 
});
</script>

<script defer src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/CodeCrumbsApp/floating-form-labels@0.1.1/index.min.js"></script>

  window.addEventListener('load', () => {
    if(window.CodeCrumbs) {
      window.CodeCrumbs.FloatLabels({ inputSelector: ".input", labelActiveClass: "float" });
    }
  });
</script> <script>
        // Get references to the input field and the element to add the class
        const searchInput = document.querySelector('.is-search-input');
        const cardsWrap = document.querySelector('.home-cards-cms-wrap');

        // Add an event listener to the input field to detect changes
        searchInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
            // Check if the input field is filled
            if (searchInput.value.trim() !== '') {
                // Add the "is-active" class to the element
                cardsWrap.classList.add('is-active');
            } else {
                // Remove the "is-active" class if the input is empty
                cardsWrap.classList.remove('is-active');
            }
        });
    </script>
